		add_political_power = 50
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = money_reserves
					value = 27.5
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = money_reserves
				value = 27.5
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = national_debt
				value = 27.5
			}
		}
	}
}

	relative_position_id = GER_two_steps_back
	prerequisite = {
		focus = GER_point_out_external_threats
	}
	x = 3
	y = 3
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus GER_completely_coincidental_pay_increases"
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = money_reserves
					value = 27.5
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = money_reserves
				value = 27.5
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = national_debt
				value = 27.5
			}
		}
		tno_pensions_improve = yes
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = GER_the_third_dimension_of_war

	cost = 2
	relative_position_id = GER_the_wehermacht_and_germany
	prerequisite = {
		focus = GER_the_wehermacht_and_germany
	}
	x = 9
	y = 2
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus GER_the_third_dimension_of_war"
		#event

			country_event = {
				id = GER_gfour_military.7
				days = 1
			}
		}
		air_experience = 15
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = GER_g4_luftwaffe_reform
		}
		set_variable = {

##### Reconvergence #####
shared_focus = {
	id = GER_a_united_front
	icon = GFX_GER_g4_a_united_front_hands
	cost = 2
	relative_position_id = GER_our_last_best_hope_for_peace
	prerequisite = {
		focus = GER_order_through_strength
		focus = GER_international_court_of_justice
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = GER_peacekeeping_mandate
		focus = GER_forum_of_nations
	}
	x = 1
	y = 11
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus GER_a_united_front"
		#the KN stands together regardless of structure
		#mechanics from previous choice come into full effect
		#coherence +1 #trust +1 #popularity +1
		add_to_variable = {
			global.GER_kn_trust = 1
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = GER_g4_kn_trust
			VALUE = {
				localization_key = TNO_COLOR_GREEN
				VALUE_CHANGE = "+1"
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = GER_a_council_of_guardians
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ZZZ_superpower
				}
				set_country_flag = ZZZ_kn_voting_member
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
					set_variable = {
						GER_kn_player_opinion = 0
					}
				}
			}
			GER = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
					set_variable = {
						GER_kn_player_opinion = 10
					}

					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
						GER = {
							NOT = {
								GER_nsdap_government = yes
							}
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						GER_kn_player_opinion = 5
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
						has_idea = ZZZ_detente_with_ger
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						GER_kn_player_opinion = 10
					}
				}
			}
			JAP = {

						GER = {

						}

					set_variable = {

				}
				else_if = {

					}
				}

						has_idea = ZZZ_detente_with_ger
					}
					add_to_variable = {

	icon = GFX_GER_g4_guide_the_pakt_eastern_europe
	cost = 5
	relative_position_id = GER_gfour_rsp_eternal_sunshine
	prerequisite = {
		focus = GER_gfour_reward_the_wehrmacht
	}
	x = 2
	y = 3
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus GER_gfour_guide_the_pakt"
		#every ally gets 10% stab, war support and +10000 manpower
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ally_with = GER
			}
			add_stability = 0.1
			add_war_support = 0.1
			add_manpower = 10000
		}
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = GER_gfour_rectify_the_wrongs
	icon = GFX_GER_An_Apology
	cost = 5
	relative_position_id = GER_gfour_vaaf_the_impossible
	prerequisite = {
		focus = GER_gfour_vaaf_the_impossible
	}
	x = -1
	y = 1
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus GER_gfour_rectify_the_wrongs"
		#increase turnout in every state
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = GER_reichstag_state
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				GER_state_turnout = 0.15
			}
			clamp_variable = {
				var = GER_state_turnout
				min = 0
				max = 1
			}
		}
		GER_reichstag_calculate_state_seats = yes
	}
}
